- **audit-type-debt**: Measure how much of the code is typed for the changed files, a path, or the whole tree, per file and as a percentage per lane: `type-coverage`'s ratio of identifiers whose type is not `any` for TypeScript, and mypy's `--any-exprs-report` coverage over expressions for Python.
